Several national governments and two [international bodies](18201224.md) have created lists of organizations that they designate as [terrorist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorist).[^cite_note-1] The following **list of designated terrorist groups** is composed of groups on whom a "terrorist" designation had been officially issued, whether by current or former national governments, or inter-governmental organizations.

Many organizations that have been designated as terrorist have denied using [terrorism](30636.md) as a [military tactic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Military_tactics) to achieve their goals, and there is no international consensus on the legal [definition of terrorism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Definition_of_terrorism).[^cite_note-Williamson2009-2][^cite_note-Schmid2011-3]

This listing does not include unaffiliated individuals accused of terrorism, which is considered [lone wolf terrorism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lone_wolf_attack). This list also excludes groups who might be widely considered terrorist but were not officially designated as such.

## Organizations designated as terrorist

## Process of designation

Among the countries that publish a list of designated terrorist organizations, some have a clear established procedure for listing and delisting, and some are opaque. The Berghof Foundation argues that opaque delisting conditions reduce the incentive for the organization to abandon terrorism, while fuelling radicalism.[^cite_note-552]

### Australia

Main article: [Designated terrorist organisations in Australia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Designated_terrorist_organisations_in_Australia)

Since 2002, the [Australian Government](1222540.md) maintains a list of terrorist organizations under the *Security Legislation Amendment (Terrorism) Act 2002*. Listing, de-listing and re-listing follows a protocol[^cite_note-au-list-18] that mainly involves the [Australian Security Intelligence Organisation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Australian_Security_Intelligence_Organisation) and the [Attorney-General's Department](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Attorney-General's_Department_\(Australia\)).[^cite_note-553]

### Bahrain

Main article: [Organizations designated as terrorist by Bahrain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Organizations_designated_as_terrorist_by_Bahrain)

The [Ministry of Foreign Affairs](18478684.md) maintains a public list of designated terrorist individuals and entities.[^cite_note-bahrain-list-8]

### Canada

Main article: [Organizations designated as terrorist by Canada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Organizations_designated_as_terrorist_by_Canada)

Since 18 December 2001, section 83.05 of the Canadian [*Criminal Code*](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Criminal_Code_\(Canada\)) allows the [Governor in Council](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Governor_in_Council) to maintain a list of entities that are engaged in terrorism, facilitating it, or acting on behalf of such an entity.[^cite_note-canada-law-554]

A review is conducted every five years by the [Minister of Public Safety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Minister_of_Public_Safety_and_Emergency_Preparedness) to determine whether an entity should remain listed. Entities may apply for a judicial review by the [Chief Justice of the Federal Court](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federal_Court_of_Canada#Judges). Both ministerial and judicial reviews are published in the *[Canada Gazette](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Canada_Gazette)*.[^cite_note-canada-law-554] The list is also published on the website of [Public Safety Canada](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Public_Safety_Canada).[^cite_note-canada-list-9]

### China

|  | This article needs to be **updated**. Please help update this article to reflect recent events or newly available information. *(January 2019)* |
| --- | --- |

In 2003, the [Ministry of Public Security](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Public_Security_\(China\)) published a list of "East Turkestan" terrorist organizations on its website [mps.gov.cn](https://web.archive.org/web/20140531105604/http://www.mps.gov.cn/n16/n983040/n1988498/1988553.html). This list was translated to English by the [Embassy of China in the US](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Embassy_of_People's_Republic_of_China_in_Washington,_D.C.).[^cite_note-china-185]

### European Union

Main article: [List of organisations designated as terrorist by the European Union](60454399.md)

The European Union has two lists of designated terrorist organisations that provide for different sanctions for the two groups.[^cite_note-555] The first list is copied from the United Nations, and the second is an autonomous list.[^cite_note-berghof-foundation.org-95] As of 13 January 2020, there are 21 organizations in the autonomous list.[^cite_note-eu-list-193]

### India

Main article: [List of organisations banned by the Government of India](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_organisations_banned_by_the_Government_of_India)

Under the [Unlawful Activities (Prevention) Act](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Unlawful_Activities_\(Prevention\)_Act), the [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Home_Affairs_\(India\)) maintains a list of banned organizations.[^cite_note-india-60]

### Iran

Main page: [Category\:Organisations designated as terrorist by Iran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Category\:Organisations_designated_as_terrorist_by_Iran)

The state maintains a list of designated terror groups; it includes the [US Armed Forces](32212.md), [CENTCOM](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CENTCOM), [Iran International](58555280.md).[^cite_note-556]

### Israel

The Israeli list of "Terrorists Organizations and Unauthorized Associations" is available at the National Bureau for Counter Terror Financing of Israel.[^cite_note-557]

### Kazakhstan

Main article: [List of organizations designated as terrorist by Kazakhstan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_organizations_designated_as_terrorist_by_Kazakhstan)

The [Government of Kazakhstan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Kazakhstan) publishes a list of terrorist organizations banned by courts.[^cite_note-kz-list-67]

### Kyrgyzstan

[Kyrgyzstan](170131.md) maintains a consolidated list of "destructive, extremist and terrorist" organizations officially banned by courts. As of February 9, 2026, the list includes 20 organizations.[^cite_note-kg-list-68][^cite_note-558]

### Malaysia

The [Ministry of Home Affairs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ministry_of_Home_Affairs_\(Malaysia\)) of [Malaysia](3607937.md) maintains a sanction list of individuals and organizations involved in terrorist activity. The list is regulated by the Anti-Money Laundering, Anti-Terrorism Financing and Proceeds From Illegal Activities Act 2001 and is complementary to the [United Nations Security Council](31956.md) sanction lists.[^cite_note-559][^cite_note-560][^cite_note-my-list-20]

### Myanmar

In [Myanmar](19457.md) (formerly *Burma*), the *Anti-Terrorism Central Committee* is responsible for designating terrorist organisations in accordance with the country's counter-terrorism law. Designations must be approved by the [union government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assembly_of_the_Union) before being official.[^cite_note-561] There are only two groups on Myanmar's terror list: the [Arakan Rohingya Salvation Army](52247136.md) and [Arakan Army](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arakan_Army_\(Kachin_State\)), declared on 25 August 2017[^cite_note-arsa-222] and January 2019.[^cite_note-562][^cite_note-563]

### New Zealand

The [New Zealand Police](406756.md) are responsible for coordinating any requests to the Prime Minister for designation as a terrorist entity. The designation of terrorist organizations is also guided by the [Terrorism Suppression Act 2002](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorism_Suppression_Act_2002). New Zealand also abides by several United Nations resolutions dealing with counter-terrorism including [UN Resolutions 1267](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1267), [1989](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1989), [2253](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_2253), [1988](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1988), and [1373](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1373).[^cite_note-564][^cite_note-nz-un-list-12][^cite_note-nz-list-200]

### Pakistan

Government of Pakistan under section 11-B of Anti Terrorism Act can declare an organization believed to be concerned with terrorism as a Proscribed Organization or put it under surveillance. Ministry of Interior issues the formal notification of proscription of an organization. [National Counter Terrorism Authority](44837043.md) is primarily concerned with monitoring for any signs of re-emergence through intelligence coordination, once an organization is proscribed.

### Philippines

Main article: [List of organizations designated as terrorist by the Philippines](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_organizations_designated_as_terrorist_by_the_Philippines)

The [Department of Foreign Affairs](4718612.md) publishes a list of designated terrorist organizations under the Human Security Act or the Terrorism Financing Prevention and Suppression Act of 2012.[^cite_note-ph-list-272]

The passage of the [Anti-Terrorism Act of 2020](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Terrorism_Act_of_2020) automatically recognized all terrorist group designations by the [United Nations](31769.md) under Philippine law which includes the [Islamic State of Iraq and the Levant](9087364.md) (ISIL).[^cite_note-565] Under the same law, the Anti-Terrorism Council was formed to designate groups as terrorists.[^cite_note-566]

### Russia

Main article: [List of organizations designated as terrorist or extremist by Russia](57212865.md)

A single federal list of organizations recognized as terrorist is used by the Supreme Court of the Russian Federation. The [National Anti-Terrorism Committee](385990.md) maintains a list of terrorist organizations, named "Federal United list of Terrorist Organizations".[^cite_note-russia-72]

### Sri Lanka

Sri Lanka bans using the ['Prevention of Terrorism (Temporary Provisions) Act](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prevention_of_Terrorism_Act_\(Sri_Lanka\)), No. 48 of 1979 regulations, cited as the Prevention of Terrorism (Proscription of Extremist Organizations).

### Tajikistan

The [National Bank of Tajikistan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/National_Bank_of_Tajikistan) publishes national lists of individuals and organizations declared terrorist or extremists by the [Supreme Court](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Supreme_Court_of_Tajikistan).[^cite_note-tj-list-74]

In 2015, the [Islamic Renaissance Party of Tajikistan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_Renaissance_Party_of_Tajikistan) was banned in [Tajikistan](30108.md) as a terrorist organization.[^cite_note-567]

### United Arab Emirates

The [Cabinet of the United Arab Emirates](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cabinet_of_the_United_Arab_Emirates) periodically issues resolutions to include individuals and organizations on its terrorist list. As of 4 March 2020, issued resolutions are 2014/41,[^cite_note-uae-2014-41-568] 2017/18,[^cite_note-uae-2017-18-569] 2017/28,[^cite_note-uae-2017-28-570] 2017/45,[^cite_note-uae-2017-45-571] 2018/24[^cite_note-uae-2018-24-572] and 2018/50.[^cite_note-uae-2018-50-573]

### United Nations

See also: [United Nations Security Council Resolution 1267](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1267) and [ISIL (Da'esh) and Al-Qaida Sanctions Committee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ISIL_\(Da'esh\)_and_Al-Qaida_Sanctions_Committee)

The United Nations does not have a list of all terrorist organizations. Instead, it has several lists focusing on [international sanctions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_sanctions) in particular contexts.[^cite_note-574] The [United Nations Security Council Resolution 1267](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Security_Council_Resolution_1267) established lists focused [Al-Qaeda](1921.md), the [Taliban](30635.md) and their associates. The listing process was later extended to include the [Islamic State of Iraq and the Levant](9087364.md).[^cite_note-un-qd-4][^cite_note-un-qd-list-6]

### United Kingdom

Main article: [Terrorism Act 2000](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Terrorism_Act_2000)

The [Secretary of State](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Secretary_of_State_\(United_Kingdom\)) can proscribe organisations. The [United Kingdom](31717.md) [Home Office](225695.md) maintains a list of proscribed groups, which also contains the proscription criteria.[^cite_note-homeoffice-16]

### United States

Main article: [United States Department of State list of Foreign Terrorist Organizations](421835.md)

The [United States Department of State](31975.md) maintains a [list of Foreign Terrorist Organizations](421835.md).[^cite_note-us-fto-17]
